========================================**--- Prologue ---**
The seventh floor of corporate headquarters smelled of industrial carpet cleaner, stale roast coffee, and impending dread. Above the endless sea of gray felt cubicles, the fluorescent lighting hummed at a specific, maddening frequency designed to erode morale. A solitary motivational poster—a soaring eagle beneath the bold header *SYNERGY*—peeled slightly at the corner, clinging to drywall that had absorbed a decade of passive-aggressive sighs and microwave-reheated salmon.

It had started with a calendar invite. Sent at 4:58 PM on a Friday with no attached agenda, the cryptic placeholder titled *Operational Alignment & Optimization Update* had instantly sent shockwaves through the department. Rumors swirled faster than the sputtering fan in the executive washroom. External management consultants had reportedly been spotted in the lower lobby, brandishing sleek aluminum clipboards, color-coded spreadsheets, and the terrifyingly polite smiles of professionals tasked with trimming departmental fat. 

For the moment, keycards still beeped green at the security turnstiles, and the breakroom water cooler maintained its gentle, oblivious bubble. The company Slack channels remained eerily quiet, save for a few nervous thumbs-up reactions to an automated birthday announcement. Yet the tension was palpable across every ergonomic task chair. Restructuring was imminent, the budget was bleeding, and someone was bound to leave with their worldly belongings stuffed into a single cardboard box.


**--- Preparation ---**
The 8:30 AM chime signaled nothing more catastrophic than an auto-scheduled calendar reminder, but the Seventh Floor collectively flinched anyway. In the kitchenette, LisaMVP eyed the Keurig’s blinking maintenance light with profound weariness, while Panda and Torqez exchanged guarded, tight-lipped nods across the partition. Nobody wanted to draw undue attention to their deliverables. Down the aisle, the faint, frantic clatter of mechanical keyboards filled the air as Arby3 printed out a seventy-page slide deck on cross-functional alignment that nobody had requested.

Near the water cooler, You_Fool and Jealous hovered in hushed conference, parsing the latest memo from senior leadership with the solemn gravity of wartime codebreakers. The consulting team had yet to make their first official strike, but the eerie, humming stillness felt heavier than a mandatory team-building exercise. Morblnde nervously tweaked their LinkedIn privacy settings, while Schniepel frantically marked hundreds of unread emails as read to present a clean desktop. 

For the moment, the department held its collective breath. Every keycard still flashed green at the glass turnstiles, not a single cardboard box had materialized on an empty desk, and HR remained cordially silent behind frosted glass. Yet, as HydroP and Vamp locked eyes over the droning copy machine, everyone understood the truce was temporary: the corporate restructuring had begun, and the first review was imminent.


**--- Night 1 ---**
The turnstiles chimed with a sharp, red rejection tone at 8:02 AM. By the time Metrex and TheBigOne poured their first cups of lukewarm dark roast, the grim reality had already settled over the Seventh Floor. Two cubicles sat completely hollowed out, stripped of ergonomics and personality alike, leaving behind only faint dust outlines where dual-monitor stands once stood.

A pair of flat-packed banker’s boxes had made their swift delivery under cover of darkness. Ordos had been intercepted before sunrise, their single sign-on credentials unceremoniously revoked and their keycard confiscated at the security barrier. Down the corridor, Jealous met an identical corporate fate—a polite exit interview via an automated HR video, a generic severance packet, and a quiet escort past the reception desk.

Both Ordos and Jealous were confirmed to be ordinary, blameless **Desk Workers**, faithful cogs who had merely wanted to clear their backlog and survive the fiscal quarter. As thirdrock nervously refreshed their own company portal and Squatch stared fixedly at an unopened spreadsheet, the chilling truth rippled through the department: the consulting team had officially begun their restructuring purge, and tenure offered zero protection.

**--- Mechanical Summary ---**
- 🔪 **Ordos** was killed in the night by Godfather. They were the **Plain Townie**.
- 🔪 **Jealous** was killed in the night by Serial Killer. They were the **Plain Townie**.

**--- Day 1 ---**
The mandatory 3:00 PM “Departmental Alignment Sync” was convened in the primary conference room under the unforgiving glare of an overheating overhead projector. Genesis and Soul nervously clicked their ballpoint pens as slide after slide outlined redundant overhead and flagging quarterly outputs. Polite corporate veneer cracked into pointed finger-pointing, and when the anonymous 360-degree feedback results finalized on screen, the department had made its ruling: Jets’ calendar was scrubbed clean, and their single sign-on access was revoked with immediate effect.

Security promptly materialized to oversee the offboarding. Into a solitary cardboard box, Jets surrendered their laminated badge, a novelty mug, and several pristine non-disclosure agreements. An immediate audit of the corner desk confirmed the room’s deepest suspicions: Jets had not been a regular spreadsheet jockey, but the department’s dreaded **Restructuring Partner (Godfather)** orchestrating the corporate purge from the shadows.

Yet the floor’s collective sigh of relief was cut short before the breakroom Keurig could even finish brewing. Across the encrypted leadership Slack channels, an automated notification pinged the remaining consulting faction. The corporate vacuum left by Jets’ termination was immediately resolved; an anonymous successor quietly stepped into the corner office, inheriting full administrative privileges to greenlight the next wave of layoffs.

**--- Mechanical Summary ---**
In the mafia underground, a power vacuum has been filled. A new leader has risen to command the night's dark deeds.
- 💀 **Jets** was lynched. They were **Godfather**.

**--- Night 2 ---**

**--- Conclusion ---**
A profound silence settled over the sixth floor, broken only by the low electric hum of fluorescent tubes reflecting off dozens of empty monitor screens. Across the vast, open-plan wasteland, rows of abandoned swivel chairs sat parked beside unplugged desk fans and a disquietingly spotless breakroom sink. Every regular desk worker had been systematically stripped of their credentials, handed a severance packet, and escorted out through the lobby. 

Inside the glass-walled boardroom overlooking the hollowed-out bullpen, the architects of the restructuring convened for a final, self-congratulatory debrief. thirdrock casually reviewed the balance sheet on the projector, thoroughly satisfied that operational headcount expenses had dropped to absolute zero. Beside them, Arby3 stamped approval on the expedited transition bonuses, while WhoFlungDung drafted the final corporate memo promoting all three to permanent senior executive retainers with generous equity packages. No one remained to submit a Jira ticket, submit an HR grievance, or question the quarterly budget ever again.

With the floor thoroughly sanitized and all resistance completely purged, the consulting mandate has reached its ruthless, flawless conclusion. Corporate Downsizing (Mafia) has won the game!
